<div class="sales-funnel-whois__message" data-dynamic-message="" data-toggle-item="" data-whois-privacy-message="">

<p>Interested in contacting the holder of this domain name? <div class="card-text__content card-text__content--align-center">
<div class="text-long"><h2><span class="red">We are Canada’s internet. </span></h2>
<p>We are the Canadian Internet Registration Authority. We manage .CA domains on behalf of all Canadians and work every day to build a better online Canada.</p>
